~~~python
            if any(s[k[1]] for k in I.T if k[2] == j):
                return False
        return True
    return False

def dep(I, e):
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    t = e['t']
    for r in sorted(runs, key=lambda r: -r['u']):
        if r['u'] <= t and not same_event(r['evs'], e):
            return 1, f"LOC 拍{r['u']} {','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in r['ks'])}←{r['lab']}"
    if not pins:
        return 0, '无可用位'
    if covered(I, e, first) and all(pins[l]['first_flip'] is None or pins[l]['first_flip'] > t for l in ('hi', 'lo')):
        return 0, '引理 C（两钉位至该拍无门控翻转）'
    return COV, '钉位翻转或种类不在引理 C 覆盖内，所试设定未改变该事件'

def path(I, e):
    R = I.reach()
    return 1 if (e['reads'] & R[e['t']]) else 0

def eta(I, e):
    """η（或 κ）的判值与依据。返回 (值, 依据, Dep 值, Path 值, 超定标记)。"""
    p = path(I, e)
    if p == 0:
        d = (0, '无途径')
        return 0, '无途径（引理 R3）', 0, 0, False
    dv, why = dep(I, e)
    if dv == 1:
        return 1, why, 1, 1, False
    if dv == 0:
        return 0, '超定：有途径而无依赖（' + why + '）', 0, 1, True
    return COV, why, dv, 1, False

def ko_act(G, o, evs, eid, eps=EPS):
    """引理 A：KO 认证的 a。返回 (a, KO 差)。"""
    e = evs[eid]; k = e['tgt']
    if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP':
        return None, None
    if k[0] != 'w':
        d = e['c']
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    i, j = k[1], k[2]
    terms = [x['c'] for x in evs.values() if x['tgt'] == k and x['id'][0] != 'CLIP']
    S = o[0][i][j] + sum(terms)
    S_ko = S - e['c']
    clip = lambda z: min(G.hi[i][j], max(G.lo[i][j], z))
    inside = lambda z: G.lo[i][j] <= z <= G.hi[i][j]
    d = clip(S) - clip(S_ko)
    if inside(S) and inside(S_ko):
        return (1 if abs(d) > eps else 0), d
    return (1 if abs(d) > eps else SAT), d

def evaluate(I, part_all=False):
    """逐项求值本篇判据。part_all=True 时 Part 逐一求出全部 B 内事件的 κ（用于统计），否则遇见证即止。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    rows, hd, hd_cons = [], [], []
    n_over = 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] not in T:
            continue
        a, d = ko_act(G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])
        if a is None:
            a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0   # 夹限事件不作敲除对象；其作用由分项判（分项判据，一般式 105 行）
        ev, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        n_over += over and a == 1
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, d=d, eta=ev, why=why, dep=dv, path=pv, over=over))
        hd.append(k_and(a, ev))
        hd_cons.append(k_and(a, '⊥_Over' if over else ev))
    part, part_cons, wit, nk = [], [], None, 0
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['car'] != RW.B:
            continue
        kv, why, dv, pv, over = eta(I, e)
        nk += 1
        part.append(kv); part_cons.append('⊥_Over' if over else kv)
        if kv == 1 and wit is None:
            wit = (e['id'], why)
            if not part_all:
                break
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    q = {k: (int(zk(G, end, k) > EPS), int(bool(I.form[k])), zk(G, end, k)) for k in T}
    qv = k_and(*[k_and(r, f) for (r, f, _) in q.values()])
    trace = k_and(RDEF, qv, 1, 1)      # 本求值器不含 r 的 R 层规则（r 由附录 A.14 求）；Ident（POS）、HistEv（REC）由模型结构为 1
    out = dict(rows=rows, HDyn=k_or(hd), HDyn_cons=k_or(hd_cons), Part=k_or(part), Part_cons=k_or(part_cons),
               Part_wit=wit, n_over=n_over, q=q, qv=qv, Trace=trace)
    out['Q'] = k_and(trace, out['Part'], out['HDyn'])
    runs, pins, first = I.cf()
    out['pins'] = {l: pins[l]['first_flip'] for l in pins} if pins else {}
    out['nruns'] = len(runs)
    out['Wmax_z'] = {k: max(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    out['Wmin_z'] = {k: min(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)) for k in T}
    return out

# ---------------- 第三篇在本实例上的判词路径 ----------------
def third_path(I, nonF):
    """按《沉积场判定式》v1.0 条文逐级求第三篇可给的判词。nonF：nonf_encoding 对本宿载的结论（True 表示无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码）。"""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    end = I.pre[I.t1 + 1]
    allpos = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    steps = []
    steps.append('Y₂：SEP 要求「宿载属 F 类」（〔第三篇〕184）；机制级申报入口要求「律属 F 类」（181）。'
                 + ('本宿载无合 BEAT、LOC 的精确 F 编码（命题 N1、N2），SEP 不能如实申报，Y₂ 判词不取得；同实例乙「反馈律不属 F 类三种数值律，定理六不适用（U9）。故只能用 Y₁」（639）。'
                    if nonF else 'F 编码存在，Y₂ 可用（本实例不出现此支）。'))
    steps.append('若强行以 488 型编码（发生按实际申报、常数律）通过入口：EF4 不成立（485），定理七（二）负判不成立；LOC 响应与编码的空支持冲突，属 E25（554）。其 F 层读数见 F 编码栏。')
    q = {}
    for k in T:
        q[k] = 0 if zk(G, end, k) <= EPS else PROF
    steps.append('Y₁（4.5，241）：q(x) 窗末 s(x) ≤ ε 判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → ' + str({f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}': q[k] for k in T}))
    steps.append('Y₁（242）：r 判 ⊥_Prof（定理五（二））')
    low = all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in T)
    ph = 0 if low else PROF
    steps.append(f'Y₁（243）：Part、HDyn 在「W 内 T 各位恒不大于 ε」时判 0，否则 ⊥_Prof → W 内 T 恒 ≤ ε：{low} ⇒ Part = HDyn = {ph}')
    steps.append('Y₁（244）：Act(m)、HistCond(m) 无清单判 ⊥_Undef；（245）Fld 判 ⊥_Prof')
    fld_neg = all(zk(G, end, k) <= EPS for k in allpos) or all(zk(G, I.pre[t], k) <= EPS for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2) for k in allpos)
    steps.append('Y₁ 加申报（247）：q = 1 须「F 类申报与 REC」——F 类申报不可得，q 仍 ⊥_Prof；'
                 f'（248）Fld 退化负判须 Dom5 且窗末无位 > ε 或 W 内一切位恒 ≤ ε：{fld_neg}；'
                 '（249）r 须 PE（196）——信号经教学电流门控界内可塑性，PE 不成立；'
                 '（250）Part 须 AC（198）——输出发放不是纯读取过程（发放写 y 并门控 PLUS），AC 不成立；'
                 '（251）HDyn 须 SAM 与 LOC 且 F 类（引理十，494）——F 类不成立')
    fld = 0 if fld_neg else PROF
    return dict(q=q, r=PROF, Part=ph, HDyn=ph, Fld=fld, steps=steps)

# ---------------- 488 型 F 编码的读数（D10—D19） ----------------
def f_layer(I, positions, ext_entry, eps=EPS):
    """入口拍 t0−1 以申报事件给出 z(t0)；W 内每一作用于位集的可塑性事件按实际时刻编码为常数律机制（发生已申报，cert_eff = 1）。
    ext_entry=True 时 T 位的入口事件记为界外脉冲（E2），否则为 B 内常数律机制（E1）。按 D12、D15—D19、4.2 求值。"""
    G, T, t0, t1 = I.G, I.T, I.t0, I.t1
    s = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
    form, mark = {k: [] for k in positions}, {k: [] for k in positions}
    dom_err = None
    for k in positions:
        z0 = zk(G, I.pre[t0], k)
        if z0 == 0:
            continue
        if z0 < 0:
            dom_err = dom_err or f'入口拍位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 的 z(t0) = {fr(z0)} < 0，越出 F 定义域（D10、D20）'
            continue
        if ext_entry and k in T:
            if z0 > eps: form[k].append(t0 - 1); mark[k].append(t0 - 1)
        elif z0 > eps:
            form[k].append(t0 - 1)
        s[k] = z0
    devs, atoms = [], []
    for t in range(t0, t1 + 1):
        incr = {k: F(0) for k in positions}
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] in positions and e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX', 'CLIP'):
                c = e['c']
                act = 1 if abs(c) > eps else 0          # D18 Act_F
                hc = 0                                  # 常数律 Supp_act = ∅（D15）⇒ HistCond_F = 0（D18 后说明）
                atoms.append(dict(id=e['id'], t=t, tgt=e['tgt'], c=c, Act=act, HistCond=hc, Cond=0))
                if act:
                    if s[e['tgt']] <= eps < s[e['tgt']] + c:
                        form[e['tgt']].append(t)
                    incr[e['tgt']] += c                 # 4.2：只入账有效分项
        for k in positions:
            s[k] += incr[k]
            if s[k] < 0 and dom_err is None:
                dom_err = f'拍 {t} 位 w{k[1]}{k[2]} 更新值 {fr(s[k])} < 0（D20）'
        for k in positions:
            if s[k] != zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k):
                devs.append((t, k, s[k] - zk(G, I.pre[t + 1], k)))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[k] for k in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[k] > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    trace = k_and(Src, Ret, 1, 1)
    part = k_or([a['Cond'] for a in atoms])
    hdyn = k_or([k_and(1 if a['tgt'] in T else 0, a['Act'], a['HistCond']) for a in atoms])
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Trace=trace, Part=part, HDyn=hdyn, Q=k_and(trace, part, hdyn),
                atoms=atoms, devs=devs, dom_err=dom_err)
~~~

A.6　decision.py　𝒟₁ 精确判定与 Part、HDyn 判词（与《候选宿载场具化判定式》v1.0 附录 A.6 逐字相同）

~~~python
"""门控类 R 层判定器（正文 5.2、5.3 与第六节）：𝒟₁ 上函数依赖的精确判定（定理 D1）、Part 与 HDyn 判词与三档负向（定理 D2）、T_max 覆盖（定理 TM）。
声明的干预域：𝒟₁（单拍联合 LOC，σ_k ∈ [b_k, hi_k]）；𝒟_m（多拍）只有引理 C、C′ 的充分负判。
夹限个别化：饱和的位—拍按「分列」与「联合（D9）」两读法各求，一致则取之，否则 ⊥_Ind。
只用标准库，精确有理运算。运行：python3 decision.py（输出含「用时」行，随机器变化，其余确定）
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import lp_exact as LP
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
# ---------- 仿射式：(常数, {变量: 系数}) ----------

def A0(c):
    return (F(c), {})

def avar(name):
    return (F(0), {name: F(1)})

def aadd(*xs):
    c, d = F(0), {}
    for x0, x1 in xs:
        c += x0
        for k, v in x1.items():
            d[k] = d.get(k, F(0)) + v
    return (c, {k: v for k, v in d.items() if v != 0})

def ascale(a, s):
    return (a[0] * s, {k: v * s for k, v in a[1].items() if v * s != 0})

def is_const(a):
    return not a[1]

def le0(a):      # a ≤ 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], False)

def lt0(a):      # a < 0
    return (dict(a[1]), a[0], True)

class Stats:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lp = 0
        self.nodes = 0

ST = Stats()

def feas(R):
    ST.lp += 1
    return LP.feasible(R)

def simplify(R):
    """同方向约束只留最紧者（系数向量按最大绝对值归一）。等价变形，不改变可行集（正文引理 L3）。"""
    best = {}
    for coef, const, strict in R:
        m = max(abs(v) for v in coef.values())
        key = tuple(sorted((k, v / m) for k, v in coef.items()))
        c = const / m
        old = best.get(key)
        if old is None or c > old[0] or (c == old[0] and strict and not old[1]):
            best[key] = (c, strict)
    return [(dict(k), c, s_) for k, (c, s_) in best.items()]

def split(R, conds):
    """conds：[(标签, [约束])]；返回可行的 (标签, R + 约束)。常值约束直接求值。"""
    out = []
    for lab, cs in conds:
        add, ok = [], True
        for c in cs:
            if not c[0]:
                v = c[1]
                if (c[2] and not v < 0) or (not c[2] and not v <= 0):
                    ok = False
                    break
            else:
                add.append(c)
        if not ok:
            continue
        R2 = simplify(R + add) if add else R
        if add:
            f, _ = feas(R2)
            if not f:
                continue
        out.append((lab, R2))
    return out

def sym_step(G, st, R, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """定义 2 的符号一拍。st = (w{(i,j): 仿射}, v[仿射], x[常], y[常])。返回 [(R′, st′, 事件{id: 仿射 c}, 净增量{(i,j): 仿射})]。"""
    w, v, x, y = st
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = [aadd(ascale(v[j], G.lam[j]), *[w[(i, j)] for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]], A0(I[j])) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    gate_opts = [[]]
    regs = [R]
    combos = [([], R)]
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        nxt = []
        for gs, Rg in combos:
            d = aadd(vt[j], A0(-G.theta[j]))      # vt − θ
            for lab, R2 in split(Rg, [(1, [le0(ascale(d, -1))]), (0, [lt0(d)])]):
                nxt.append((gs + [lab], R2))
        combos = nxt
    out = []
    for g, Rg in combos:
        v2 = [A0(G.vreset[j]) if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        x2 = [G.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)]
        y2 = [G.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(G.n_out)]
        ev0 = {}
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            ev0[('LEAK', (j,))] = ascale(v[j], G.lam[j] - 1)
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                if s[i]:
                    ev0[('SYN', (i, j))] = w[(i, j)]
            if I[j] != 0:
                ev0[('INJ', (j,))] = A0(I[j])
            if g[j]:
                ev0[('RESET', (j,))] = aadd(A0(G.vreset[j]), ascale(vt[j], -1))
                ev0[('YIN', (j,))] = A0(1)
            ev0[('YDEC', (j,))] = A0((G.dy[j] - 1) * y[j])
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            ev0[('XDEC', (i,))] = A0((G.dx[i] - 1) * x[i])
            if s[i]:
                ev0[('XIN', (i,))] = A0(1)
        Ssum = {}
        for i in range(G.n_in):
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                parts = [w[(i, j)]]
                if plastic and bap and g[j]:
                    c = A0(G.Ap * x2[i]); ev0[('PLUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and bap and s[i]:
                    c = A0(-G.Am * y2[j]); ev0[('MINUS', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                if plastic and G.rho != 0:
                    c = ascale(aadd(w[(i, j)], A0(-G.b[i][j])), -G.rho); ev0[('RELAX', (i, j))] = c; parts.append(c)
                Ssum[(i, j)] = aadd(*parts)
        pieces = [({}, Rg)]
        for k, S in Ssum.items():
            lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
            nxt = []
            for asg, Rp in pieces:
                opts = [('lo', [lt0(aadd(S, A0(-lo)))]),                                       # S < lo
                        ('mid', [le0(aadd(A0(lo), ascale(S, -1))), le0(aadd(S, A0(-hi)))]),     # lo ≤ S ≤ hi
                        ('hi', [lt0(aadd(A0(hi), ascale(S, -1)))])]                              # S > hi
                for lab, R2 in split(Rp, opts):
                    a2 = dict(asg); a2[k] = lab
                    nxt.append((a2, R2))
            pieces = nxt
        for asg, Rp in pieces:
            ev = dict(ev0)
            w2, net = {}, {}
            for k, S in Ssum.items():
                lab = asg[k]
                lo, hi = G.lo[k[0]][k[1]], G.hi[k[0]][k[1]]
                if lab == 'mid':
                    w2[k] = S
                else:
                    b = A0(lo if lab == 'lo' else hi)
                    w2[k] = b
                    ev[('CLIP', k)] = aadd(b, ascale(S, -1))
                net[k] = aadd(w2[k], ascale(w[k], -1))
            out.append((Rp, (w2, v2, x2, y2), ev, net))
    return out

def point_of(R, names):
    ok, x = feas(R)
    assert ok
    return {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}

def differs(R, a, c0):
    """R 上是否存在点使仿射 a ≠ c0；返回见证点或 None。"""
    if is_const(a):
        if a[0] != c0:
            ok, x = feas(R) if R else (True, {})
            return x
        return None
    for cons in (lt0(aadd(a, A0(-c0))), lt0(aadd(A0(c0), ascale(a, -1)))):
        ok, x = feas(simplify(R + [cons]))
        if ok:
            return x
    return None

# ---------- 实例与精确判定 ----------
class Decider:
    def __init__(self, I, budget=200000):
        self.I, self.G = I, I.G
        self.budget = budget
        self.sat = {}            # (k, t) 饱和的 T 位—拍 → 实际净增量
        for t in range(len(I.hist)):
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' and e['tgt'] in I.T:
                    k = e['tgt']
                    self.sat[(k, t)] = I.pre[t + 1][0][k[1]][k[2]] - I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]
        self.fd1 = {}            # 事件 id → (u, 见证 dict) ；只记判 1 者
        self.fd1_net = {}
        self.complete_u = {}     # u → 是否在预算内穷尽
        self.verify_fail = 0
        self._run()
    def avail_T(self, u):
        return [k for k in self.I.T if self.I.is_avail(k, u)]
    def targets(self):
        I = self.I
        return {e['id'] for e in I.Wevents()}
    def _run(self):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        tgt = self.targets()
        us = [u for u in range(0, I.t1 + 1) if self.avail_T(u)]
        for u in us:
            S = self.avail_T(u)
            names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
            w0 = {(i, j): A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
            R0 = []
            for k, nm in zip(S, names):
                w0[(k[1], k[2])] = avar(nm)
                R0.append(le0(aadd(A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), ascale(avar(nm), -1))))      # b − σ ≤ 0
                R0.append(le0(aadd(avar(nm), A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))               # σ − hi ≤ 0
            st0 = (w0, [A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
            nodes = 0
            stack = [(u, st0, R0)]
            complete = True
            while stack:
                t, st, R = stack.pop()
                nodes += 1
                if nodes > self.budget:
                    complete = False
                    break
                for R2, st2, ev, net in sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw):
                    for eid_s, e in I.evs[t].items():
                        if eid_s not in tgt or eid_s in self.fd1:
                            continue
                        key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        if key not in ev:
                            wit = point_of(R2, names)
                        else:
                            x = differs(R2, ev[key], e['c'])
                            wit = None if x is None else {n: x.get(n, F(0)) for n in names}
                        if wit is not None:
                            self._verify(u, S, names, wit, e)
                            self.fd1[eid_s] = (u, {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    for (k, tt), net0 in self.sat.items():
                        if tt == t and (k, t) not in self.fd1_net and t >= I.t0:
                            x = differs(R2, net[(k[1], k[2])], net0)
                            if x is not None:
                                self.fd1_net[(k, t)] = (u, {kk: x.get(nm, F(0)) for kk, nm in zip(S, names)})
                    if t + 1 <= I.t1:
                        stack.append((t + 1, st2, R2))
            ST.nodes += nodes
            self.complete_u[u] = complete
    def _verify(self, u, S, names, wit, e):
        I, G = self.I, self.G
        assign = {k: wit[nm] for k, nm in zip(S, names)}
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], assign), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        if GK.same_event(evs, e):
            self.verify_fail += 1
    def fd1_value(self, e):
        if e['id'] in self.fd1:
            return 1
        us = [u for u in self.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
        return 0 if all(self.complete_u[u] for u in us) else GK.COV

def path_act(I, sources, e):
    """实际事件图（D13，只含实际发生的事件；读边、写边、结构位持存边）上自 sources = {(结点, 拍)} 是否可达 e。"""
    if not sources:
        return 0
    reach = set()
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        reach |= {k for (k, u) in sources if u == t}
        if t == e['t']:
            return 1 if (e['reads'] & reach) else 0
        nxt = {k for k in reach if k[0] == 'w'}
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            if ev['reads'] & reach:
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
        reach = nxt
    return 0

def eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """返回 dict：值、档位、依据。domain='D1' 用 𝒟₁ 精确判定；'Dm' 另用引理 C、C′ 与钉位见证。"""
    if GK.path(I, e) == 0:
        return dict(v=0, tier='a', why='Path_slot = 0（R3）', fd=0)
    fd = D.fd1_value(e)
    wit = D.fd1.get(e['id'])
    if domain == 'Dm' and fd != 1:
        dv, why = GK.dep(I, e)
        if dv == 1:
            fd, wit = 1, None
        elif dv == 0 and fd == 0:
            fd = 0
        else:
            fd = GK.COV
    if fd == 1:
        if wit is not None:
            u, asg = wit
            src = {(k, u) for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            why = f'𝒟₁ 见证 拍{u} ' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}←{fr(val)}' for k, val in asg.items())
        else:
            src = {(k, u) for k in I.T for u in I.avail[k] if u <= e['t']}
            why = '钉位见证（𝒟_m）'
        if path_act(I, src, e):
            return dict(v=1, tier='+', why=why + '；实际图可达', fd=1)
        return dict(v='⊥_Open', tier='+?', why=why + '；实际图不可达（依赖只经未发生支路）', fd=1)
    if fd == 0:
        return dict(v=0 if bfd else '⊥_Over', tier='b', why=f'FD_{domain} = 0（全部分支不可行或常值相等）' + ('' if bfd else '；未申报 B_FD'), fd=0)
    return dict(v=GK.COV, tier='c', why='预算内未穷尽', fd=GK.COV)

def judge(I, D, bfd=True, domain='D1'):
    """Part、HDyn 判词（分列读法与联合读法；二者一致取之，否则 ⊥_Ind）。"""
    T = I.T
    rows = []
    hd_sep, hd_joint, pt_sep, pt_joint = [], [], [], []
    satk = {(k, t) for (k, t) in D.sat if t >= I.t0}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        ex = eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd, domain)
        a = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
        ko = GK.ko_act(I.G, I.pre[e['t']], I.evs[e['t']], e['id'])[0]
        rows.append(dict(e=e, a=a, ko=ko, **ex))
        if e['tgt'] in T:
            hd_sep.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
            if (e['tgt'], e['t']) not in satk:
                hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ex['v']))
        if e['car'] == RW.B:
            pt_sep.append(ex['v'])
            if not (e['tgt'] in T and (e['tgt'], e['t']) in satk):
                pt_joint.append(ex['v'])
    for (k, t) in satk:      # 联合事件：c = 净增量，读取 = 诸成员之并
        net0 = D.sat[(k, t)]
        mem = [e for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] == k]
        reads = frozenset().union(*[e['reads'] for e in mem])
        pseudo = dict(id=('NET', (k[1], k[2]), t), t=t, reads=reads, tgt=k, car=RW.B, c=net0)
        if net0 == 0:
            a = '⊥_Joint' if any(e['c'] != 0 for e in mem) else 0
        else:
            a = 1 if abs(net0) > EPS else 0
        if GK.path(I, pseudo) == 0:
            ev = 0
        elif (k, t) in D.fd1_net:
            u, asg = D.fd1_net[(k, t)]
            src = {(kk, u) for kk, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][kk[1]][kk[2]]}
            ev = 1 if path_act(I, src, pseudo) else '⊥_Open'
        else:
            ev = 0 if bfd else '⊥_Over'
        hd_joint.append(GK.k_and(a, ev)); pt_joint.append(ev)
    hs, hj, ps, pj = GK.k_or(hd_sep), GK.k_or(hd_joint), GK.k_or(pt_sep), GK.k_or(pt_joint)
    H = hs if hs == hj else '⊥_Ind'
    P = ps if ps == pj else '⊥_Ind'
    return dict(rows=rows, HDyn=H, Part=P, HDyn_sep=hs, HDyn_joint=hj, nsat=len(satk))

def premises(I, D, res, bfd):
    """判 0 的前件清单（EF1—EF5 的对应物，正文 5.3）。"""
    base = ['MOD-R（R1：事件清单完备，模型结构）', 'R2/R3（读取集与槽位图闭合，模型结构）', 'REC', 'ENT', 'POS', 'BEAT-R（模型拍即 L 拍、ε 申报）']
    tiers = {r['tier'] for r in res['rows'] if r['v'] == 0}
    if 'b' in tiers:
        base += ['DOM[𝒟₁]（干预域事前申报）', 'B_FD[𝒟₁]（函数依赖读法的对象层对应）']
    if res['nsat']:
        base += ['IND_CLIP（夹限个别化，两读法一致才不需要）']
    return base

# ---------- 报告 ----------
def show(I, title, bfd=True, verbose=True):
    t0 = time.time()
    D = Decider(I)
    res = judge(I, D, bfd)
    resn = judge(I, D, False)
    print(f'【{title}】{I.name}')
    print(f'  W = 拍 {I.t0}—{I.t1}；T 首个可用拍 {min(min(I.avail[k]) for k in I.T if I.avail[k])}；'
          f'𝒟₁ 起点拍数 {len(D.complete_u)}，均在预算内穷尽：{all(D.complete_u.values())}；见证具体重跑核验失败 {D.verify_fail}；饱和位—拍 {len(D.sat)}')
    if verbose:
        print('  作用对象属 T 的 W 内事件：事件@拍 | c | a（读出/KO） | η | 档位 | 依据')
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in I.T:
                print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {r["a"]}/{r["ko"]} | {r["v"]} | {r["tier"]} | {r["why"]}')
    cnt = {}
    for r in res['rows']:
        if r['e']['car'] == RW.B:
            cnt[(r['tier'], str(r['v']))] = cnt.get((r['tier'], str(r['v'])), 0) + 1
    print('  B 内事件 κ 的档位分布：', '；'.join(f'{t}:{v}×{n}' for (t, v), n in sorted(cnt.items())))
    print(f'  判词（申报 B_FD[𝒟₁]）：Part = {res["Part"]}，HDyn = {res["HDyn"]}（分列 {res["HDyn_sep"]}／联合 {res["HDyn_joint"]}）')
    print(f'  判词（不申报 B_FD）：Part = {resn["Part"]}，HDyn = {resn["HDyn"]}')
    if res['HDyn'] == 0 or res['Part'] == 0:
        print('  判 0 的前件清单：', '；'.join(premises(I, D, res, bfd)))
    print(f'  （用时 {time.time() - t0:.1f} 秒）')
    return D, res, resn

def random_validation(n_inst=40, seed=7, samples=60):
    """定理 D1 的有限核验：随机 K_g 成员与史；对每一判 FD₁ = 0 的事件，在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样设定，均不改变该事件；
    对每一判 1 者见证已具体重跑。另与 gate_criteria 的有限设定判据对照（后者判 1 ⇒ 此处判 1）。"""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(inst=0, events=0, fd0=0, fd1=0, samp=0, viol=0, vfail=0, gk1_not1=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['hi'] = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2, 3]), 2)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        form = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 3)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(3, 7))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
        st['inst'] += 1; st['vfail'] += D.verify_fail
        for e in I.Wevents():
            v = D.fd1_value(e)
            st['events'] += 1
            if v == 1:
                st['fd1'] += 1
            elif v == 0:
                st['fd0'] += 1
                us = [u for u in D.complete_u if u <= e['t']]
                for _s in range(samples // 10 if us else 0):
                    u = rng.choice(us)
                    asg = {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 48), 48) * (G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]) for k in D.avail_T(u)}
                    _, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg), I.hist[u:e['t'] + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
                    st['samp'] += 1
                    if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                        st['viol'] += 1
            dv, _ = GK.dep(I, e)
            if dv == 1 and v != 1:
                # gate_criteria 的钉位见证属 𝒟_m，可在 𝒟₁ 之外；只统计其单拍见证不被 𝒟₁ 判 1 的情形
                runs, _, _ = I.cf()
                single = any((not r['lab'].startswith('钉位')) and r['u'] <= e['t'] and not GK.same_event(r['evs'], e) for r in runs)
                st['gk1_not1'] += single
    return st

def tmax_coverage():
    """T_max 归约的判词覆盖（正文定理 TM）：形成史使 4 个入口突触都成迹应，T_max = 4 位；
    对 T_max 的一切非空子集求 Part、HDyn，核单调性（T ⊆ T′ ⇒ 判词按真值序不减，且 1、0 不翻转）。"""
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    hist = [('AB', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0)), NONE, ('B', (F(1, 2), 0)), NONE, ('A', (F(1, 4), 0))]
    allT = [('w', i, 0) for i in range(4)]
    base = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=allT)
    end = base.pre[len(hist)]
    Tmax = [k for k in allT if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and base.form[k]]
    print(f'  史 (AB+教学 1, 静, 静, A, 静, B+教学 1/2, 静, A+教学 1/4)，W = 拍 3—7；T_max = {[f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Tmax]}')
    res = {}
    for r in range(1, len(Tmax) + 1):
        for Ts in itertools.combinations(Tmax, r):
            I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, 3, T=list(Ts))
            D = Decider(I, budget=50000)
            j = judge(I, D, True)
            res[Ts] = (j['Part'], j['HDyn'], all(D.complete_u.values()), D.verify_fail)
    order = {0: 0, '⊥': 1, 1: 2}
    viol = 0
    for Ta in res:
        for Tb in res:
            if set(Ta) < set(Tb):
                for idx in (0, 1):
                    va, vb = res[Ta][idx], res[Tb][idx]
                    if va in (0, 1) and vb in (0, 1) and va > vb:
                        viol += 1
                    if va == 1 and vb != 1:
                        viol += 1
                    if vb == 0 and va != 0:
                        viol += 1
    for Ts, v in sorted(res.items(), key=lambda kv: len(kv[0])):
        print(f'    T = {{{",".join(f"w{k[1]}{k[2]}" for k in Ts)}}}：Part = {v[0]}，HDyn = {v[1]}，穷尽 {v[2]}，见证核验失败 {v[3]}')
    print(f'  子集对 T ⊂ T′ 上单调性违例 {viol}（1 在扩大时保持为 1，0 在缩小时保持为 0）')
    return viol

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 冻结申报史', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+教学 1, B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 申报史，W = (A+教学 1, 静, 静)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('形成 1 次；入口拍 A+教学；W = (B+教学 9/16, 静, 静, 静, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2、A₊ = 1/4；形成 (A+教学, 静, 静)×6，静置 8，W 为 (A,静,静,B,静,静)×3', Gs,
                      FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    print('一、实例判词（𝒟₁ 精确判定；HistCond 三档负向；分列/联合两读法）')
    for I, t in ((P, '实例 P'), (N, '实例 N'), (O1, '实例 O1'), (O2, '实例 O2'), (SAT, '实例 SAT（夹限生效）')):
        show(I, t, verbose=(I is not SAT))
        print()
    print('二、定理 D1 的有限核验（随机 K_g 成员与史；FD₁ = 0 的事件在 𝒟₁ 内随机抽样均不变；判 1 的见证已具体重跑）')
    st = random_validation()
    print(f'  实例 {st["inst"]}，W 内事件 {st["events"]}：FD₁ = 1 者 {st["fd1"]}、= 0 者 {st["fd0"]}；抽样 {st["samp"]} 次，违例 {st["viol"]}；'
          f'见证重跑失败 {st["vfail"]}；有限设定判据的单拍见证未被 𝒟₁ 判 1 者 {st["gk1_not1"]}')
    print('三、T_max 归约的判词覆盖')
    tmax_coverage()
    print(f'四、线性可行性调用合计 {ST.lp} 次；符号分支结点合计 {ST.nodes}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.7　common12.py　m 族与读数约定（与《沉积动力具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.4 逐字相同）

~~~python
"""《沉积动力具化数理式》附录程序·公共定义（只用标准库，精确有理运算）。
本模块实现与宿载无关的部分：
  共同水平函数 m 的合法条件（《沉积动力一般式》v1.2 附件一第五节，片段 B5：事先申报、幂等 m(c,…,c) = c、居间、对称）
  m_α 族：m_α(δ_K) = min + α(max − min)，α ∈ [0, 1] ∩ ℚ（引理 M1：合法 m 的 c* 值域恰为 [min, max] ∩ ℚ）
  三类相对读数：首批开放集合 Y_first、先后次序 Y_order、份额 Y_share（片段 B4「读数可以是首批开放者、先后次序或份额」）
  成员居先 Lead_q（独先／同先／不先，《沉积动力一般式》v1.2 附件一第五节原名）
  B_m、B_m^q 的判值（片段 B5）；读数未定义时记 'undef-reading'，I_m 未定义时记 'undef-Im'，均不判假
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import itertools
INF = None   # 未开放的时刻记 None（即 +∞）

# ---------- 共同水平函数 m ----------
def m_min(ds):
    return min(ds)

def m_max(ds):
    return max(ds)

def m_mean(ds):
    return sum(ds, F(0)) / len(ds)

def m_alpha(alpha):
    alpha = F(alpha)
    assert 0 <= alpha <= 1
    def m(ds):
        return min(ds) + alpha * (max(ds) - min(ds))
    m.__name__ = f'm_alpha({alpha})'
    return m

M_STD = {'min': m_min, 'mean': m_mean, 'max': m_max}

def check_m_legal(m, samples):
    """合法四条件中可机检的三条：幂等、居间、对称（事先申报由流程保证）。samples 为若干沉积度组。"""
    for ds in samples:
        ds = [F(d) for d in ds]
        c = ds[0]
        if m([c] * len(ds)) != c:
            return False, ('幂等', ds)
        v = m(ds)
        if not (min(ds) <= v <= max(ds)):
            return False, ('居间', ds)
        for p in itertools.permutations(ds):
            if m(list(p)) != v:
                return False, ('对称', ds)
    return True, None

# ---------- 读数 ----------
def y_first(times):
    """times: {path: 首次开放拍或 None}。返回首批开放集合（W 内无开放时为空集）。"""
    fin = [t for t in times.values() if t is not None]
    if not fin:
        return frozenset()
    t0 = min(fin)
    return frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == t0)

def y_order(times):
    """先后次序：W 内开放者按首次开放拍的弱序（并列成组），未开放者另列。只读相对次序，不读绝对拍。"""
    fin = sorted(set(t for t in times.values() if t is not None))
    groups = tuple(frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t == tt) for tt in fin)
    never = frozenset(p for p, t in times.items() if t is None)
    return (groups, never)

def y_share(counts):
    """份额：counts {path: 通过量}，总量为 0 时读数未定义（同场篇第十一 FlowShare 的定义域条件，片段 C2）。"""
    tot = sum(counts.values(), F(0))
    if tot == 0:
        return 'undef'
    return tuple(sorted((p, F(c) / tot) for p, c in counts.items()))

def lead(reading_kind, Y, q):
    """成员居先 Lead_q ∈ {独先, 同先, 不先}；份额读数未定义时返回 'undef'。"""
    if reading_kind == 'first':
        if Y == frozenset([q]):
            return '独先'
        return '同先' if q in Y else '不先'
    if reading_kind == 'order':
        groups, never = Y
        if not groups or q not in groups[0]:
            return '不先'
        return '独先' if len(groups[0]) == 1 else '同先'
    if reading_kind == 'share':
        if Y == 'undef':
            return 'undef'
        d = dict(Y)
        mx = max(d.values())
        tops = [p for p, v in d.items() if v == mx]
        if q not in tops:
            return '不先'
        return '独先' if len(tops) == 1 else '同先'
    raise ValueError(reading_kind)

def B_value(Y0, Y1):
    """B_m 判值：I_m 已定义时调用。任一侧读数未定义记 'undef-reading'（不判假）。"""
    if Y0 == 'undef' or Y1 == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return Y0 != Y1

def Bq_value(kind, Y0, Y1, q):
    a, b = lead(kind, Y0, q), lead(kind, Y1, q)
    if a == 'undef' or b == 'undef':
        return 'undef-reading'
    return a != b

def fmt(x):
    if isinstance(x, F):
        return str(x)
    if isinstance(x, frozenset):
        return '{' + ','.join(str(e) for e in sorted(x, key=str)) + '}'
    if isinstance(x, tuple):
        return '(' + ', '.join(fmt(e) for e in x) + ')'
    return str(x)
~~~

A.8　fam\_jia\_wu.py　族甲、族戊物理机制模型与 I\_m（与《沉积动力具化数理式》v1.0 附录 A.6 逐字相同）

~~~python
"""宿载乙、丙：构造式族甲（恒压头两通道流网）与族戊（发放率神经网络）上的 I_m、B_m（份额读数）、F、Ω_W、Bias。
运行：python3 fam_jia_wu.py
物理机制模型逐项按《场具化构造式》v1.0 4.2、4.3 的本构前件与参数（该篇 4.2、4.3 节）重写，精确有理运算：
  族甲：Qᵢ = (g₀ + κ_构 zᵢ)ΔP；写入 φcQᵢΔt/ℓ；弛豫 −γzᵢΔt 入库 Lᵢ；库存输出 min(Lᵢ, eQᵢΔt)（拍首库存）；seed 向通道 1 投放 2 A_u。
  族戊：yᵢ = β(w₀ + zᵢ)x；写入 ηxyᵢΔt；衰减 −γzᵢΔt；受体池 Rᵢ −=（写入 + 诱导 − 衰减）/g₁；pair 向突触 1 诱导 +2 S_u。
记号：《场具化构造式》C1 的导通系数 κ 与场篇第一定义 11 的续行势 κ 同名异义，本篇记前者为 κ_构。
申报扩充（标「扩」，不属《场具化构造式》原申报）：族甲⁺ 增冻结阻断标志 brkᵢ（brkᵢ = 1 时 gᵢ = 0，通道不导通）。
读数：份额 Y_share = W 内 ΣQᵢ（族甲）或 Σyᵢ（族戊）之比；两族无出流阈，首批开放与先后次序读数无对象（场篇第一方程 4、定理 17 无直接对象）。
I_m（路径层模型甲，路径 = 通道／突触，Depth = zᵢ）：z₁ = z₂ = c*；冻结 Lᵢ、brk、输入协议；族戊受体池按 N5 守恒随动重算（回写规则 R-池，见正文 10.4）。
"""
import sys, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from common12 import y_share, lead, B_value, Bq_value, m_min, m_max, m_mean, m_alpha, fmt
# ---------------- 族甲 ----------------
JIA = dict(g0=F(1), kap=F(1), c=F(1, 5), phi=F(1, 2), ell=F(1), gam=F(1, 10), e=F(1, 5), zmax=F(10))

def jia_step(st, letter, P=JIA, brk=(0, 0)):
    z, L = list(st['z']), list(st['L'])
    dP = F(1) if letter == 'flow' else F(0)
    Q = [(0 if brk[i] else (P['g0'] + P['kap'] * z[i])) * dP for i in range(2)]
    exp_ = [min(L[i], P['e'] * Q[i]) for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['phi'] * P['c'] * Q[i] / P['ell'] for i in range(2)]
    rel = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - rel[i] for i in range(2)]
    L2 = [L[i] + rel[i] * P['ell'] - exp_[i] for i in range(2)]
    if letter == 'seed':
        z2[0] += 2
    assert all(0 <= v <= P['zmax'] for v in z2) and all(v >= 0 for v in L2), '越出适用域'
    return dict(z=z2, L=L2), Q

def jia_run(st, hist, P=JIA, brk_sched=None):
    Qs = []
    for t, a in enumerate(hist):
        brk = brk_sched[t] if brk_sched else (0, 0)
        st, Q = jia_step(st, a, P, brk)
        Qs.append(Q)
    return st, Qs

# ---------------- 族戊 ----------------
WU = dict(w0=F(1), beta=F(1), eta=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), g1=F(1, 10), ymax=F(100))

def wu_step(st, letter, P=WU):
    z, R = list(st['z']), list(st['R'])
    x = F(1) if letter == 'drive' else F(0)
    y = [P['beta'] * (P['w0'] + z[i]) * x for i in range(2)]
    wr = [P['eta'] * x * y[i] for i in range(2)]
    dec = [P['gam'] * z[i] for i in range(2)]
    ind = [F(2) if (letter == 'pair' and i == 0) else F(0) for i in range(2)]
    z2 = [z[i] + wr[i] - dec[i] + ind[i] for i in range(2)]
    R2 = [R[i] - (wr[i] + ind[i] - dec[i]) / P['g1'] for i in range(2)]
    phys = all(v >= 0 for v in R2) and all(v <= P['ymax'] for v in y) and all(v >= 0 for v in z2)
    return dict(z=z2, R=R2), y, phys

def wu_run(st, hist, P=WU):
    ys, ok = [], True
    for a in hist:
        st, y, phys = wu_step(st, a, P)
        ys.append(y); ok = ok and phys
    return st, ys, ok

# ---------------- 读数与 I_m ----------------
def share_of(series, beats=None):
    beats = range(len(series)) if beats is None else beats
    return y_share({i + 1: sum((series[t][i] for t in beats), F(0)) for i in range(2)})

def Im_jia(st, m):
    c = m(st['z'])
    ext = sum((c - zi) * JIA['ell'] for zi in st['z'])       # 与 B_phys 之外交换的结构物质体积（正为加入）
    return dict(z=[c, c], L=list(st['L'])), c, ext

def Im_wu(st, m, rule='pool'):
    """rule='pool'：受体池按 N5 守恒随动（R 为由沉积度决定之量）；rule='frozen'：池冻结（违反 N5 时记物理不合法）。"""
    c = m(st['z'])
    if rule == 'pool':
        R = [st['R'][i] - (c - st['z'][i]) / WU['g1'] for i in range(2)]
        feasible = all(r >= 0 for r in R)
    else:
        R = list(st['R'])
        feasible = all(c == zi for zi in st['z'])               # 池冻结而电导改变即违反受体守恒 N5
    return dict(z=[c, c], R=R), c, feasible

MFAM = [m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4)), m_alpha(F(3, 4))]

def thmJ1_check():
    """定理 J-1 / W-1 的有限核验：族甲（κ_构 ≥ 0，0 ≤ γ ≤ 1，W 内只含 flow、rest）与族戊（W 内只含 drive、rest）：
       份额读数有定义 ⇔ W 内有导通拍；有定义时 B_m ⇔ κ_eff > 0 ∧ ∃ 导通拍 t，d(t) ≠ 0；与 m 无关；I_m 后 z₁ ≡ z₂。"""
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n = viol = 0
    zs = [F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1), F(2), F(5)]
    for kap in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)):
        for gam in (F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2), F(1)):
            P = dict(JIA, kap=kap, gam=gam)
            for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
                for _ in range(6):
                    L = rng.randint(1, 6)
                    hist = [rng.choice(['flow', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
                    st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P)
                    Y0 = share_of(Q0)
                    # 预测
                    d = z1 - z2
                    pred_true = False
                    for a in hist:
                        if a == 'flow':
                            if kap > 0 and d != 0:
                                pred_true = True
                            d = d * (1 + P['phi'] * P['c'] * kap - gam)
                        else:
                            d = d * (1 - gam)
                    defined = 'flow' in hist
                    for m in MFAM:
                        c = m([z1, z2])
                        st1 = dict(z=[c, c], L=[F(0), F(0)])
                        st_end, Q1 = jia_run(st1, hist, P)
                        Y1 = share_of(Q1)
                        B = B_value(Y0, Y1)
                        n += 1
                        if not defined:
                            viol += (B != 'undef-reading')
                        else:
                            viol += (B != pred_true)
                        viol += (st_end['z'][0] != st_end['z'][1])
    nw = vw = 0
    for z1, z2 in itertools.product(zs, zs):
        for _ in range(6):
            L = rng.randint(1, 6)
            hist = [rng.choice(['drive', 'rest']) for _ in range(L)]
            st0 = dict(z=[z1, z2], R=[F(100), F(100)])
            _, y0, _ = wu_run(st0, hist)
            Y0 = share_of(y0)
            d = z1 - z2
            pred = False
            for a in hist:
                if a == 'drive':
                    pred = pred or d != 0
                    d = d * (1 + WU['eta'] * WU['beta'] - WU['gam'])
                else:
                    d = d * (1 - WU['gam'])
            for m in MFAM:
                st1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
                _, y1, _ = wu_run(st1, hist)
                B = B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))
                nw += 1
                vw += (B != ('undef-reading' if 'drive' not in hist else pred))
    return n, viol, nw, vw

def subsegments(n_beats, k):
    size = n_beats // k
    return [list(range(i * size, (i + 1) * size)) for i in range(k)]

def relations_jia(label, st0, hist, brk_sched=None, P=JIA, k=3, eps_d=F(1, 100), m=m_mean, q=0):
    """在族甲（或族甲⁺）上求 D、B_m（份额）、B_m^q、F(q)、Ω_W(q)、Bias(q 对另一路)。
    申报：子段 k 等分；Pass = 导通拍数（Qᵢ > 0）；熟径常走判据与 Ω 复走判据同取 Pass ≥ k；Ω 第三项为各子段都有导通拍；
    Brk_W(q) = 1 当且仅当 W 内某拍 brk_q = 1；Bias：(4a) W 起点 z_q − z_o > ε_δ，(4b) 每个子段 q 的流量份额 > 1/2（长窗稳定偏续的操作化）。"""
    o = 1 - q
    segs = subsegments(len(hist), k)
    st, Qs = jia_run(dict(st0), hist, P, brk_sched)
    Y0 = share_of(Qs)
    stI, c, ext = Im_jia(st0, m)
    _, QsI = jia_run(stI, hist, P, brk_sched)
    B = B_value(Y0, share_of(QsI))
    D = st0['z'][0] != st0['z'][1]
    passes = sum(1 for Q in Qs if Q[q] > 0)
    fam = st0['z'][q] > 0 and passes >= k
    # F：逐子段比较任务 T_k，起点状态取实际运行
    Fok = fam
    cur = dict(st0)
    seg_lead = []
    for sg in segs:
        sub = [hist[t] for t in sg]
        sub_brk = [brk_sched[t] for t in sg] if brk_sched else None
        _, Qk = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
        Yk = share_of(Qk)
        cI, _, _ = Im_jia(cur, m)
        _, QkI = jia_run(cI, sub, P, sub_brk)
        YkI = share_of(QkI)
        ld = lead('share', Yk, q + 1)
        bq = Bq_value('share', Yk, YkI, q + 1)
        seg_lead.append((ld, bq))
        Fok = Fok and ld == '独先' and bq is True
        cur, _ = jia_run(dict(cur), sub, P, sub_brk)
    brkq = any(b[q] for b in brk_sched) if brk_sched else False
    seg_pass = all(any(Qs[t][q] > 0 for t in sg) for sg in segs)
    Om = passes >= k and not brkq and seg_pass
    a4 = st0['z'][q] - st0['z'][o] > eps_d
    b4 = all(sum((Qs[t][q] for t in sg), F(0)) > sum((Qs[t][o] for t in sg), F(0)) for sg in segs)
    Bias = a4 and b4
    return dict(label=label, D=D, B=B, F=Fok, Omega=Om, Bias=Bias, a4=a4, b4=b4, fam=fam, seg=seg_lead, Y0=Y0)

def main():
    print('一、族甲、族戊的 I_m 实现（路径层模型甲：路径 = 通道／突触，Depth = zᵢ）')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], L=[F(1, 5), F(0)])          # 《场具化构造式》H1 驱动拍之后的状态（5.1）
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, ext = Im_jia(st, m)
        print(f'  族甲 z = (21/10, 1/10)，{m.__name__}：c* = {c}，回写唯一（z₁ = z₂ = c*），库存 L 冻结；与 B_phys 外交换结构物质 {ext}（正为加入）')
    print('  族甲物理实现：《场具化构造式》申报的界外作用只有向通道 1 定量投放 2 A_u（C5）；I_m 一般需要「加入或取出任意量」的界外设定操作，须另申报（扩）；')
    print('  申报后质量账以界外交换项闭合，c* ∈ [min, max] ⊂ [0, z_max]，不越适用域。')
    st = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], R=[F(19), F(39)])           # 《场具化构造式》H1 族戊：池 40 → 20 → 19；突触 2 驱动一拍写 1/10、池 39
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        s2, _, feas2 = Im_wu(st, m, 'frozen')
        print(f'  族戊 z = (21/10, 1/10)，R = (19, 39)，{m.__name__}：c* = {c}；R-池随动后 R = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]})，R-池资源可行 {feas}；池冻结读法资源可行 {feas2}')
    st = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(5)])
    print('  族戊见证（形式良定而 R-池资源不可行）：z = (2, 0)，R = (20, 5)：')
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max, m_alpha(F(1, 4))):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st, m, 'pool')
        print(f'    {m.__name__}：c* = {c}，R′ = ({s1["R"][0]}, {s1["R"][1]})，R-池资源可行 {feas}')
    print('    R-池资源可行条件（定理 W-2；不表示已有合法的物理设定途径，后者须申报扩充操作）：c* ≤ min_i [zᵢ + g₁Rᵢ]；此处 c* ≤ 0 + (1/10)·5 = 1/2，即 m_α 中 α ≤ 1/4')
    print('二、定理 J-1 / W-1（份额读数）的有限核验')
    n, viol, nw, vw = thmJ1_check()
    print(f'  族甲（κ_构 ∈ {{0, 1/2, 1}}，γ ∈ {{0, 1/10, 1/2, 1}}，z 对 36，随机 flow/rest 史 6 条，m 5 个）：判值 {n}，违例 {viol}')
    print(f'  族戊（z 对 36，随机 drive/rest 史 6 条，m 5 个）：判值 {nw}，违例 {vw}')
    print('三、族甲上 D 成立而 B_m 不成立的两种来源')
    hist = ['flow'] * 3
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0), F(0)])
    P0 = dict(JIA, kap=F(0))
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, P0); _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0)
    print('  律级（κ_构 = 0，导通不读迹应）：Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '→', fmt(share_of(Q1)), '；B_mean =', B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1)))
    brk = [(0, 1)] * 3
    _, Q0 = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA, brk)
    res = {}
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        _, Q1 = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m)[0], hist, JIA, brk)
        res[m.__name__] = B_value(share_of(Q0), share_of(Q1))
    print('  态级（扩：通道 2 阻断，冻结）：Y =', fmt(share_of(Q0)), '；B(min/mean/max) =', res)
    # 区分：改变冻结量（解除阻断）后 B 恢复；律级在任何冻结量取值下 B 都为假
    _, Q0u = jia_run(st0, hist, JIA); _, Q1u = jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, JIA)
    print('  区分检验（不是 I_m，是对冻结量的另一反事实）：解除阻断后 B_mean =', B_value(share_of(Q0u), share_of(Q1u)),
          '；κ_构 = 0 时对一切 brk ∈ {0,1}² 的 B_mean =',
          [B_value(share_of(jia_run(st0, hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]), share_of(jia_run(Im_jia(st0, m_mean)[0], hist, P0, [b] * 3)[1]))
           for b in ((0, 0), (0, 1), (1, 0))])
    print('四、族甲、族戊上 D、B_m、F、Ω_W、Bias 的情形表（m = mean，子段 3，ε_δ = 1/100；q = 通道／突触 1）')
    cases = [
        ('甲1 基准（κ_构 = 1，z = (2, 0)，flow×6）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('甲2 律级退化（κ_构 = 0）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, dict(JIA, kap=F(0))),
        ('甲3 扩：通道 2 全窗阻断', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 1)] * 6, JIA),
        ('甲4 扩：通道 1 末拍被截断', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, [(0, 0)] * 5 + [(1, 0)], JIA),
        ('甲5 等深（z = (1, 1)）', dict(z=[F(1), F(1)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
        ('甲6 单次导通（flow, rest×5）', dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] + ['rest'] * 5, None, JIA),
        ('甲7 迹应在阈下（z = (1/200, 0)，ε_δ = 1/100）', dict(z=[F(1, 200), F(0)], L=[F(0)] * 2), ['flow'] * 6, None, JIA),
    ]
    for lab, st0, hist, brk, P in cases:
        r = relations_jia(lab, st0, hist, brk, P)
        print(f'  {lab}：D={r["D"]} B={r["B"]} F={r["F"]} Ω={r["Omega"]} Bias={r["Bias"]}（4a={r["a4"]}, 4b={r["b4"]}）熟径={r["fam"]}；子段 (Lead, B^q) = {r["seg"]}')
    print('  族戊（发放率，份额读数，与族甲同一参数化编码，《场具化构造式》命题五）：')
    st0 = dict(z=[F(2), F(0)], R=[F(20), F(40)])
    hist = ['drive'] * 6
    _, y0, ok0 = wu_run(dict(st0), hist)
    Y0 = share_of(y0)
    for m in (m_min, m_mean, m_max):
        s1, c, feas = Im_wu(st0, m)
        _, y1, ok1 = wu_run(s1, hist)
        print(f'    戊1 z = (2, 0)，drive×6，{m.__name__}：Y = {fmt(Y0)} → {fmt(share_of(y1))}，B = {B_value(Y0, share_of(y1))}，I_m 的 R-池资源可行 {feas}，W 内收支 {ok1}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9　deps15.py　载入冻结依赖并核 SHA-256（5.1—5.4）

~~~python
"""第十五篇附录程序：载入冻结依赖（与本程序同目录），载入前逐个核 SHA-256。
来源（逐字取用，不改一字）：
  第十一篇 v1.0 附录程序：snn_core.py gate_class.py r_layer_witness.py gate_criteria.py lp_exact.py decision.py
  第十二篇 v1.0 附录程序：fam_jia_wu.py common12.py
"""
import sys, os, hashlib
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
DEP = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
SHA = {
    'snn_core.py': '0c750fb5f57b52c40ff30feb7734c120096ffd7267e6ddc723d163cc0d1b4ceb',
    'gate_class.py': '3b9bf68fa128bc27f2600ec35eb697b466ba3248013b9e1becae3bfffd7754e4',
    'r_layer_witness.py': '50785e9f6790f3047595b9bc40b23186e781a19976ac871cf8caf41aff4cdf6f',
    'gate_criteria.py': '4db576950ed993a730f81325099195fc10ee119a0e65ed9d9c878142de8698c5',
    'lp_exact.py': '1c03a3fcc9d7a9b3065bdfcab466c6395f4e4b23b79be64a370f6712749abeda',
    'decision.py': '5aefc70dfc911e29cef1f7ca390d6ef36638266f8c8d3be9bc8c85fe8a98da81',
    'fam_jia_wu.py': '981112a678285d5c1e8edc12de3e1a19bc4eae6b4d1a6becb22b12340924d79b',
    'common12.py': '51c42b2941ebb3b190876966a12c2fc5ef249c4b9f76e52503fe8518eb6aa591',
}
for fn, h in SHA.items():
    got = hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(DEP, fn), 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    assert got == h, fn + ' 不是冻结版本'
sys.path.insert(0, DEP)
import gate_class as GC          # noqa: E402
import r_layer_witness as RW     # noqa: E402
import gate_criteria as GK       # noqa: E402
import decision as DC            # noqa: E402
import fam_jia_wu as FJW         # noqa: E402
S, H_CORE = GC.load_snn_core()

def banner():
    print('〇、冻结依赖核验：' + '；'.join(f'{fn} {h[:8]}…{h[-8:]}' for fn, h in SHA.items()) + '。全部相符。')
~~~

A.10　core15.py　公共模块：线性本位族 F 层求值、SNN 轻量原子、律层过程表与对应条件核验、转导器 τ、GS、整步交换、有效入账截取与 CQ（第五节）

~~~python
"""第十五篇《场具化职责对应数理式》附录程序：公共模块。
只用标准库，精确有理运算（fractions）。
  一、线性本位族 M(a, b, k, γ, ε) 的 R 层求值（《场具化构造式》族甲、族戊经编译 J 所得 F 模型，按《场具化数理式》D16—D19 求原子；
      另给干预读法 FD 的有限核验，与 HistCond_F 对照，即正文引理 15-0）。
  二、SNN（𝒦_g 成员）的逐拍开关运行、轻量原子（q、r 的 M1₀ 认证、SYN 的 κ、作用于 T 的有效事件），完整 Part、HDyn 用第十一篇判定器。
  三、律层过程表与五类映射 Φ、对应条件 MC1—MC5 的律层核验。
  四、主对的冻结事件转导器 τ、门控枢轴条件 GS（只设可用位）、线性聚合与瓶颈聚合的整步交换核验。
  五、F 层有效入账截取 Q 与同支条件 CQ。
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, RW, GK, DC

def fr(q):
    q = F(q)
    return str(q.numerator) if q.denominator == 1 else f'{q.numerator}/{q.denominator}'

def fx(q, n=6):
    return f'{float(q):.{n}f}'

# =====================================================================
# 一、线性本位族（两位；形成字母一个界外脉冲 a 入位 0；驱动字母每位写入 b + k·z；各拍弛豫 −γ·z）
# =====================================================================
WU = dict(name='族戊', form='pair', drive='drive', rest='rest', wr='hebb', rl='decay',
          a=F(2), b=F(1, 10), k=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), eps=F(1, 1000),
          chain_wr=('syn', 'N', 'syn'), chain_rl=('syn', 'syn'), cut='cut(N→syn)')
JIA = dict(name='族甲', form='seed', drive='flow', rest='rest', wr='write', rl='relax',
           a=F(2), b=F(1, 10), k=F(1, 10), gam=F(1, 10), eps=F(1, 1000),
           chain_wr=('bed', 'flow', 'bed'), chain_rl=('bed', 'bed'), cut='cut(flow→bed)')

def lf_events(M, s, letter, t, sw):
    """一拍的事件（拍前状态 s）。sw：该拍干预标记集合 {'cut', 'freeze', 'freeze_rl'}。"""
    ev = []
    if letter == M['form']:
        ev.append(dict(kind='pulse', i=0, t=t, c=M['a'], car='界外', k=F(0), cert=1))
    for i in range(2):
        bt, kt = (M['bsched'][t], F(0)) if t in M.get('bsched', {}) else (M['b'], M['k'])
        if letter == M['drive'] and 'freeze' not in sw and (bt != 0 or kt != 0):
            ev.append(dict(kind=M['wr'], i=i, t=t, c=bt + kt * s[i], car='B', k=kt,
                           cert=0 if 'cut' in sw else 1))
        if M['gam'] != 0 and 'freeze' not in sw and 'freeze_rl' not in sw:
            ev.append(dict(kind=M['rl'], i=i, t=t, c=-M['gam'] * s[i], car='B', k=-M['gam'], cert=1))
    return ev

def lf_eval(M, hist, W, T, eps=None, sw=None, erase=None, s0=None, scale=F(1)):
    """F 层求值（《场具化数理式》D16—D19；4.2 只入账有效分项）。scale：深度尺度 α（a、b 与 ε 同乘 α，k、γ 不变）。
    返回 dict：原子、轨迹、窗内事件（含 Act、Cond、HistCond）。"""
    eps = (M['eps'] if eps is None else eps)
    Ms = dict(M, a=M['a'] * scale, b=M['b'] * scale)
    sw = sw or {}
    erase = erase or {}
    s = list(s0) if s0 else [F(0), F(0)]
    form = {0: [], 1: []}
    mark = {0: [], 1: []}
    traj, wev = [list(s)], []
    t0, t1 = W
    for t, letter in enumerate(hist):
        for i in erase.get(t, ()):
            s[i] = F(0)
        evs = lf_events(Ms, s, letter, t, sw.get(t, set()))
        new = list(s)
        for e in evs:
            i, c = e['i'], e['c']
            eff = abs(c) > eps
            e['a'] = 1 if eff else 0
            if e['kind'] == 'pulse':
                if eff:
                    new[i] += c
                    form[i].append(t)
                    mark[i].append(t)
                continue
            avail = (i in T) and s[i] > eps and any(u < t for u in form[i])
            supp_act = e['k'] != 0
            supp_cond = supp_act or (abs(c) <= eps)
            e['hist'] = 1 if (avail and supp_act and e['cert'] == 1) else 0
            e['cond'] = 1 if (avail and supp_cond) else 0
            e['avail'] = avail
            if eff:
                if s[i] <= eps < s[i] + c:
                    form[i].append(t)
                new[i] += c
            if t0 <= t <= t1:
                wev.append(e)
        assert all(v >= 0 for v in new), '越出定义域（D20）'
        s = new
        traj.append(list(s))
    Src = 1 if any(mark[i] for i in T) else 0
    Ret = 1 if all(s[i] > eps and form[i] for i in T) else 0
    Part = 1 if any(e['cond'] for e in wev if e['car'] == 'B') else 0
    HD = [e for e in wev if e['i'] in T and e['a'] and e['hist']]
    HDyn = 1 if HD else 0
    Q = Src & Ret & Part & HDyn
    return dict(Src=Src, Ret=Ret, Part=Part, HDyn=HDyn, Q=Q, inner=HDyn, traj=traj, wev=wev, form=form, mark=mark,
                witness=[(e['kind'], e['i'], e['t']) for e in HD])

def lf_fd(M, hist, W, T, e, cap=F(10), eps=None):
    """干预读法：在 u ≤ t(e) 拍把可用的 T 位设为 [0, cap] 内若干有理点，重跑至 t(e)，事件出现或分项改变则 FD = 1（有限核验）。"""
    eps = M['eps'] if eps is None else eps
    base = lf_eval(M, hist, (0, len(hist) - 1), {0, 1}, eps)
    for u in range(0, e['t'] + 1):
        su = base['traj'][u]
        av = [i for i in T if su[i] > eps and any(v < u for v in base['form'][i])]
        for i in av:
            for val in (F(0), su[i] / 2, su[i] * 2, cap):
                if val == su[i]:
                    continue
                s0 = list(su)
                s0[i] = val
                r = lf_eval(M, hist[u:], (0, len(hist) - 1 - u), {0, 1}, eps, s0=s0)
                cand = [x for x in r['wev'] if x['kind'] == e['kind'] and x['i'] == e['i'] and x['t'] + u == e['t']]
                if not cand or cand[0]['c'] != e['c']:
                    return 1
    return 0

# =====================================================================
# 二、SNN（𝒦_g）辅助
# =====================================================================
NONE = GK.NONE
TPOS = [('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0)]

def snn_run(G, hist, sw=None, o0=None):
    """逐拍开关运行。sw：拍 → (plastic, bap)；缺省 (True, True)。返回 (拍前状态表, 事件表, 门控表)。"""
    sw = sw or {}
    o = o0 or G.init()
    pre, evs, gates = {}, {}, {}
    for t, sig in enumerate(hist):
        pl, bp = sw.get(t, (True, True))
        pre[t] = o
        evs[t] = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t, pl, bp)
        o, rec = G.step(o, sig, plastic=pl, bap=bp)
        gates[t] = rec['g']
    pre[len(hist)] = o
    return pre, evs, gates

def zk(G, o, k):
    return o[0][k[1]][k[2]] - G.b[k[1]][k[2]]

def snn_form_avail(G, pre, evs, T, n, eps):
    form = {k: [] for k in T}
    for t in range(n):
        for e in evs[t].values():
            k = e['tgt']
            if k in T and abs(e['c']) > eps and zk(G, pre[t], k) <= eps < zk(G, pre[t], k) + e['c']:
                form[k].append(t)
    avail = {k: {t for t in range(n + 1) if zk(G, pre[t], k) > eps and any(u < t for u in form[k])} for k in T}
    return form, avail

def snn_light(G, hist, t0, T=TPOS, sw=None, eps=F(1, 100)):
    """轻量原子：q；W 内作用于 T 的有效事件；SYN 承担的 κ（读可用 T 位；同拍把该位设回基线即改分项，读边可达）。"""
    pre, evs, gates = snn_run(G, hist, sw)
    n = len(hist)
    form, avail = snn_form_avail(G, pre, evs, T, n, eps)
    q = 1 if all(zk(G, pre[n], k) > eps and form[k] for k in T) else 0
    teff = [e for t in range(t0, n) for e in evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] in T and abs(e['c']) > eps]
    syn_k = [e for t in range(t0, n) for e in evs[t].values()
             if e['id'][0] == 'SYN' and ('w',) + e['id'][1] in T and t in avail[('w',) + e['id'][1]]]
    return dict(q=q, teff=teff, kappa_syn=1 if syn_k else 0, n_syn=len(syn_k), pre=pre, evs=evs, gates=gates,
                form=form, avail=avail)

def withdrawals(G, sig):
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    pats = [nm for nm, p in G.pat.items() if all(p[i] <= s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))]
    nz = [j for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != 0]
    out = []
    for nm in pats:
        for m in range(1 << len(nz)):
            I2 = list(I)
            for b, j in enumerate(nz):
                if m >> b & 1:
                    I2[j] = 0
            cand = (nm, tuple(I2))
            if cand != (name, tuple(I)):
                out.append(cand)
    return out

def snn_r_M1(G, hist, T=TPOS, eps=F(1, 100), sw=None):
    """r 的同拍容许撤除认证 M1₀（第十一篇判据 PS 的 M1₀ 一支）：作用于 T 的每一有效写入，若某同拍撤除满足无开启条件且改变其发生或分项，记认证。"""
    pre, evs, gates = snn_run(G, hist, sw)
    sw = sw or {}
    cert, tot = 0, 0
    for t in range(len(hist)):
        pl, bp = sw.get(t, (True, True))
        for e in evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in T or abs(e['c']) <= eps or e['id'][0] not in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'RELAX'):
                continue
            tot += 1
            for sig2 in withdrawals(G, hist[t]):
                _, rec = G.step(pre[t], sig2, plastic=pl, bap=bp)
                if any(rec['g'][j] > gates[t][j] for j in range(G.n_out)):
                    continue
                e2 = RW.Ev(G, pre[t], sig2, t, pl, bp).get(e['id'])
                if e2 is None or e2['c'] != e['c']:
                    cert += 1
                    break
    return dict(r=1 if cert else 0, cert=cert, tot=tot)

def snn_full(name, G, hist, t0, T=TPOS):
    """第十一篇判定器（𝒟₁ 精确判定，申报 B_FD）求 Part、HDyn；返回 (Part, HDyn, 作用于 T 的窗内事件行)。"""
    I = GK.Instance(name, G, hist, t0, T=T)
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    res = DC.judge(I, D, True)
    rows = [(r['e']['id'][0] + ''.join(map(str, r['e']['id'][1])) + '@' + str(r['e']['t']), r['e']['c'], r['a'], r['v'], r['tier'])
            for r in res['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in T]
    return res['Part'], res['HDyn'], rows, I

# =====================================================================
# 三、律层过程表、Φ 与 MC 核验
# =====================================================================
# 字段：role（+写入／−抑制／−弛豫／读出／形成／辅助）、car、tgt（T／aux）、reads_T（律读 T 位）、reads_aux（律读的未映射辅助量）、
#       mode（律层决定方式集合：occ 发生、str 强度）、law（律型）、chain（途径结点类）、off（使其不发生的干预）、cut（使其途径认证为 0 的干预）
SNN_TABLE = {
    'PLUS': dict(role='+写入', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'x', 'w_nonT'}, mode={'occ'},
                 law='门控阶跃', chain=('突触', '输出单元', '突触'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
    'MINUS': dict(role='−抑制', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'y', 'w_nonT'}, mode={'str'},
                  law='门控阶跃（y′ = d·y + g）', chain=('突触', '输出单元', '突触'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
    'SYN': dict(role='读出', car='B', tgt='aux', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='线性（k = 1）',
                chain=('突触', '输出单元'), off=set(), cut=set()),
    'FORM': dict(role='形成', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=False, reads_aux={'v', 'I', 'x'}, mode=set(), law='门控阶跃（教学配对）',
                 chain=('界外刺激', '入口单元', '输出单元', '突触'), off={'bap_off', 'plastic_off'}, cut=set()),
}
WU_TABLE = {
    'hebb': dict(role='+写入', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='线性（b + k·z）',
                 chain=('突触', '后神经元', '突触'), off={'freeze'}, cut={'cut'}),
    'decay': dict(role='−弛豫', car='B', tgt='T', reads_T=True, reads_aux=set(), mode={'str'}, law='线性（−γ·z）',
                  chain=('突触', '突触'), off={'freeze'}, cut=set()),
    'pair': dict(role='形成', car='界外', tgt='T', reads_T=False, reads_aux=set(), mode=set(), law='脉冲',
                 chain=('界外电极', '突触'), off=set(), cut=set()),
}
JIA_TABLE = {
    'write': dict(WU_TABLE['hebb'], chain=('壁', '通道载液', '壁')),
    'relax': dict(WU_TABLE['decay'], chain=('壁', '壁')),
    'seed': dict(WU_TABLE['pair'], chain=('界外投放点', '壁')),
}
NODE_MAP_MAIN = {'突触': '突触', '输出单元': '后神经元', '界外刺激': '界外电极', '入口单元': None}
NODE_MAP_ENC = {'壁': '突触', '通道载液': '后神经元', '界外投放点': '界外电极'}

def role_class(r):
    return '+' if r.startswith('+') else ('−' if r.startswith('−') else r)

def mc_check(tab1, tab2, phiE, node_map, phiI, eps1, eps2, alpha, lam1=None, premise2=None):
    """律层核验 MC1—MC5（子项）。返回 [(条款, 是否成立, 说明)]。"""
    out = []
    T1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['tgt'] == 'T' and d['role'] != '形成']
    T2 = [k for k, d in tab2.items() if d['tgt'] == 'T' and d['role'] != '形成']
    bad = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2 or role_class(tab1[k]['role']) != role_class(tab2[phiE[k]]['role'])
           or tab1[k]['car'] != tab2[phiE[k]]['car']]
    surj = set(phiE.get(k) for k in T1) >= set(T2)
    out.append(('MC1-W（窗内作用于 T 的过程：职责类、载体、对象对应；满射到对方作用于 T 的过程）', not bad and surj,
                f'不合 {bad}；满射 {surj}'))
    f1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['role'] == '形成']
    fb = [k for k in f1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2 or tab2[phiE[k]]['car'] != tab1[k]['car']]
    out.append(('MC1-F（形成过程的载体归属对应）', not fb,
                '；'.join(f'{k}: car {tab1[k]["car"]} ↦ {phiE.get(k)}: car {tab2[phiE[k]]["car"] if phiE.get(k) in tab2 else "—"}' for k in f1)))
    p1 = [k for k, d in tab1.items() if d['car'] == 'B' and d['reads_T'] and d['tgt'] != 'T']
    pb = [k for k in p1 if phiE.get(k) not in tab2]
    out.append(('MC1-P（B 内读 T 而作用于非 T 的过程有像）', not pb, f'无像 {pb}'))
    ra = [k for k in T1 if tab1[k]['reads_aux'] and phiE.get(k) in tab2 and not tab2[phiE[k]]['reads_aux']]
    out.append(('MC2-r（读取集对应：律所读的辅助量与非 T 位有像）', not ra,
                '；'.join(f'{k} 读未映射 {sorted(tab1[k]["reads_aux"])}' for k in ra)))
    mm = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) in tab2 and tab1[k]['mode'] != tab2[phiE[k]]['mode']]
    out.append(('MC2-m（律层决定方式相同）', not mm,
                '；'.join(f'{k} {sorted(tab1[k]["mode"])} ↦ {phiE[k]} {sorted(tab2[phiE[k]]["mode"])}' for k in mm)))
    lt = [k for k in T1 if phiE.get(k) in tab2 and tab1[k]['law'].split('（')[0] != tab2[phiE[k]]['law'].split('（')[0]]
    out.append(('MC2⁺ 的必要部分（律型相同）', not lt,
                '；'.join(f'{k} {tab1[k]["law"]} ↦ {tab2[phiE[k]]["law"]}' for k in lt)))
    cb = []
    for k in T1:
        k2 = phiE.get(k)
        if k2 not in tab2:
            continue
        c1, c2 = tab1[k]['chain'], tab2[k2]['chain']
        img = tuple(node_map.get(n, n) for n in c1)
        if img != c2:
            cb.append(f'{k}: {"→".join(c1)} ↦ {"→".join(map(str, img))} ≠ {"→".join(c2)}')
    out.append(('MC3（途径链结点对应、全在 B 内、时刻前向）', not cb, '；'.join(cb) or '逐结点对应'))
    for I1, I2 in phiI.items():
        dis1 = {phiE.get(k) for k in T1 if I1 in tab1[k]['off']}
        cut1 = {phiE.get(k) for k in T1 if I1 in tab1[k]['cut']}
        dis2 = {k for k in T2 if I2 in tab2[k]['off']}
        cut2 = {k for k in T2 if I2 in tab2[k]['cut']}
        ok = dis1 == dis2 and cut1 == cut2
        out.append((f'MC4（{I1} ↦ {I2}）', ok,
                    f'一侧：使不发生 {sorted(x for x in dis1 if x)}、使途径认证为 0 {sorted(x for x in cut1 if x)}；'
                    f'另一侧：使不发生 {sorted(dis2)}、使途径认证为 0 {sorted(cut2)}'))
    out.append(('MC5-ε（ε₂ = α·ε₁）', eps2 == alpha * eps1, f'ε₁ = {fr(eps1)}，ε₂ = {fr(eps2)}，α = {fr(alpha)}'))
    if lam1 is not None:
        out.append(('MC5-τ（时标前件：对方「膜时间常数 ≪ Δt」在本侧拍上成立，即 λ = 0 或有块映射实现）', lam1 == 0,
                    f'λ = {fr(lam1)}；块映射不实现见定理 15-10（MD-2）'))
    return out

# =====================================================================
# 四：冻结转导器 τ、GS、聚合的整步交换
# =====================================================================
FORM_LETTER = ('A', (F(1), 0))

def tau(hist):
    """主对的冻结事件转导器 τ（D15-5）：一位状态 f（是否已有形成像），初值 0。逐拍：
      字母恰为 (A, 教学 (1, 0))：f = 0 时输出 pair 并置 f = 1；f = 1 时输出 rest；
      其余字母中入口 0、1 同开者（A、AB，任意教学电流）输出 drive；
      其余（B、静置、只有教学电流者）输出 rest。
    输出只依字母前缀，是确定的序列函数（Mealy 机），不是逐字母的静态函数。"""
    f, out = 0, []
    for name, I in hist:
        if (name, tuple(F(x) for x in I)) == (FORM_LETTER[0], tuple(F(x) for x in FORM_LETTER[1])):
            out.append('rest' if f else 'pair')
            f = 1
        elif name in ('A', 'AB'):
            out.append('drive')
        else:
            out.append('rest')
    return out

def gs(G, o, sig, T, avail, j=0):
    """门控枢轴条件 GS(t, j)（D15-10）：把本拍被 j 读取且在 t 可用的 T 位设回基线后
    门控实参 < θ_j ≤ 实际门控实参。返回 (GS, 实际 vt, 设回后 vt)。"""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    vt = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
    vtb = G.lam[j] * v[j] + sum((G.b[i][j] if (('w', i, j) in T and ('w', i, j) in avail) else w[i][j]) * s[i]
                                for i in range(G.n_in)) + I[j]
    return vtb < G.theta[j] <= vt, vt, vtb

def split_step(M, xs, letter, a_split):
    """四位源模型（族戊的每一突触拆为两条平行子突触，律同族戊，读本位）的一拍，机制层（不截有效阈）。
    返回 (后态, 各位分项列表)。"""
    cs = [[] for _ in xs]
    if letter == M['form']:
        cs[0].append(a_split[0]); cs[1].append(a_split[1])
    for i, z in enumerate(xs):
        if letter == M['drive']:
            cs[i].append(M['b'] + M['k'] * z)
        cs[i].append(-M['gam'] * z)
    return [z + sum(c) for z, c in zip(xs, cs)], cs

def wu_mech_step(M, zs, letter, a):
    cs = [[] for _ in zs]
    if letter == M['form']:
        cs[0].append(a)
    for i, z in enumerate(zs):
        if letter == M['drive']:
            cs[i].append(M['b'] + M['k'] * z)
        cs[i].append(-M['gam'] * z)
    return [z + sum(c) for z, c in zip(zs, cs)], cs

# =====================================================================
# 五：F 层有效入账截取与聚合的交换
# =====================================================================
def Qe(c, eps):
    """F 层有效入账 Q(c) = c·[|c| > ε]（《场具化数理式》4.2）。"""
    return c if abs(c) > eps else F(0)

def cq_condition(cs, eps):
    """引理 15-1a（F 支）的同支条件 CQ：一族原像分项或全部有效且同号，或全部无效（|c| ≤ ε）。"""
    if all(abs(c) <= eps for c in cs):
        return True
    return all(abs(c) > eps for c in cs) and (all(c > 0 for c in cs) or all(c < 0 for c in cs))
~~~

A.11　run15\_main.py　入口：主对与边界对的律层核验、两侧原子、保存登记（10.13、10.16）

~~~python
"""第十五篇附录程序：主对（实例 P（s1 参数）↔ 族戊）与边界对（族甲 ↔ 族戊）上 MC1—MC5 的律层核验、两侧原子与保存登记。
运行：python3 -B run15_main.py > run15_main.txt
"""
import sys, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, GK, FJW, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
G = GC.snn_gate(GC.load_snn_core()[0])
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * 6
REST = [C.NONE] * 8
HP = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()          # 实例 P（第十一篇 7.5）

def phi_sigma(sig, form_once=False, t=None):
    """字母映射 φ_Σ（申报）：A+教学 (1,0) ↦ pair（形成）；A、AB ↦ drive（前神经元发放，限于已映射入口 0、1）；B、静置 ↦ rest。
    教学电流在形成字母以外无像（族戊无向后神经元的注入）。form_once：只把首个形成字母映为 pair，其余形成字母 ↦ rest。"""
    name, I = sig
    if name == 'A' and tuple(I) == (F(1), 0):
        return 'pair' if (not form_once or t == 0) else 'rest'
    if name in ('A', 'AB'):
        return 'drive'
    return 'rest'

print('\n一、主对申报')
print('  𝔐₁ = 第十一篇定义 4—7 的 R 层申报实例，实例 P（s1 冻结申报史：形成 (A+教学(1,0), 静, 静)×6，静置 8，W = 拍 26—43 为 (A,静,静,B,静,静)×3；'
      'T₁ = {w00, w10}，ε₁ = 1/100）。')
H2 = [phi_sigma(s) for s in HP]
H2b = C.tau(HP)                      # 冻结转导器 τ（D15-5）；断言与逐拍写法一致
assert H2b == [phi_sigma(s, True, t) for t, s in enumerate(HP)]
print('  𝔐₂ = 《场具化构造式》族戊的编码 J(χ_戊)（a = 2，b = k = 1/10，γ = 1/10，ε₂ = 1/1000），史 h₂ = φ_Σ(h₁)，T₂ = {z1}，W 同拍。')
print('  h₂ =', ' '.join(H2))
print('  Φ_main：φ_Ξ 为分叉读法的路聚合（路 0 = {w00, w10} ↦ z1，路 1 = {w01, w11} ↦ z2，Depth = 路内 z 的算术平均，深度尺度 α）；'
      'φ_E：PLUS ↦ hebb，MINUS ↦ decay，形成写入 ↦ pair，SYN 与辅助过程无像；φ_t 为恒等拍映射；'
      'φ_I：bap 关 ↦ cut(N→syn)，plastic 关 ↦ freeze，擦除（w ← b）↦ z ← 0，𝒟₁ 设定 σ ↦ 路均值设定。')
print('\n二、MC1—MC5 律层核验（主对）')
PHI_E = {'PLUS': 'hebb', 'MINUS': 'decay', 'FORM': 'pair', 'SYN': None}
PHI_I = {'bap_off': 'cut', 'plastic_off': 'freeze'}
for alpha in (F(1), F(1, 10)):
    print(f'  〔α = {C.fr(alpha)}〕')
    for name, ok, why in C.mc_check(C.SNN_TABLE, C.WU_TABLE, PHI_E, C.NODE_MAP_MAIN, PHI_I, F(1, 100), F(1, 1000), alpha,
                                    lam1=G.lam[0]):
        print(f'    {"成立" if ok else "不成立"}｜{name}｜{why}')
print('\n三、两侧原子（模型模式；SNN 以 R 层见证求值，族戊按 F 层 D16—D19）')
Lt = C.snn_light(G, HP, 26)
rM = C.snn_r_M1(G, HP)
Part1, HDyn1, rows1, I1 = C.snn_full('P', G, HP, 26)
print(f'  𝔐₁：q = {Lt["q"]}；r = {rM["r"]}（作用于 T 的有效写入 {rM["tot"]} 个，M1₀ 认证 {rM["cert"]} 个）；'
      f'Part = {Part1}（SYN 承担 κ 的事件 {Lt["n_syn"]} 个）；HDyn = {HDyn1}；Q = {Lt["q"] & rM["r"] & Part1 & HDyn1}')
print('    作用于 T 的窗内事件：' + '；'.join(f'{a} c={C.fx(c)} a={aa} η={v}({tier})' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows1))
for lab, h2 in (('h₂ = φ_Σ⁰(h₁)（静态字母函数，六次 pair）', H2), ('h₂′ = τ(h₁)（冻结转导器，只首次 pair）', H2b)):
    R2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, h2, (26, 43), {0})
    st = dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(40), F(40)])
    st, ys, ok = FJW.wu_run(st, h2)
    print(f'  𝔐₂ {lab}：Src = {R2["Src"]}，Ret = {R2["Ret"]}，Part = {R2["Part"]}，HDyn = {R2["HDyn"]}，Q = {R2["Q"]}，界内自反馈 = {R2["inner"]}；'
          f'拍 26 的 z1 = {C.fx(R2["traj"][26][0])}；HDyn 见证 {R2["witness"][:4]}…；'
          f'第十二篇 wu_run 物理核验（受体池初值 40）{"通过" if ok else "不通过"}，末态池 {[C.fx(r, 2) for r in st["R"]]}')
    fdok = all(C.lf_fd(C.WU, h2, (26, 43), {0}, e) == e['hist'] for e in R2['wev'] if e['i'] == 0 and e['avail'])
    print(f'    引理 15-0 有限核验（族戊窗内作用于 z1 且可用的事件：干预读法 FD = HistCond_F）：{fdok}')
print('\n四、主对保存登记（值相同不等于由 MC 认证；认证依据见正文定理）')
R2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, H2, (26, 43), {0})
av2 = all(e['avail'] for e in R2['wev'] if e['kind'] == 'hebb' and e['i'] == 0)
reg = [
    ('q（Ret）', Lt['q'], R2['Ret'], '状态对应 + 形成对应（近定义，不计增量）；主对无运行对应，值同不由 MC 认证'),
    ('r（Src）', rM['r'], R2['Src'], 'MC1-F 不成立；跨路线 MC3ˢ 给 1→2 的 Pres⁺（目标侧决定，定理 15-7）'),
    ('Part', Part1, R2['Part'], '不签 Part 保存；定理 15-5 只给「源 HDyn = 1 ⇒ 目标 Part = 1」（此处源 HDyn = 1 成立）；冻结对照见 run15_regress 三'),
    ('Act（逐事件）', 1, 1, 'MC2⁺ 幅度对应不成立；族戊驱动拍写入 |c| ≥ b = 1/10 > ε₂ 由律层下界给出'),
    ('HistCond（逐事件）', 1, 1, f'1→2：z1 可用即 η₂ = 1（引理 15-0，AV₂ = {av2}）；2→1：在固定 n 的纯类 𝒞₁ᵖ(n) 上按定理 15-15 分域，在给出的共同像所在纤维上歧义（定理 15-8）'),
    ('HDyn', HDyn1, R2['HDyn'], '实例 P 不属任何 𝒞₁ᵖ(n)（窗内有 B 拍）；其 τ-纤维在含 AB 的类中不纯（run15_regress 五）；值同，不签迁移'),
    ('Q', Lt['q'] & rM['r'] & Part1 & HDyn1, R2['Q'], '值同，不签迁移'),
    ('界内自反馈', 1, R2['inner'], 'MC3（PLUS↔hebb）结点 B 归属对应；随 HDyn'),
]
for a, v1, v2, why in reg:
    print(f'  {a}｜𝔐₁ = {v1}｜𝔐₂ = {v2}｜{why}')
print('\n五、边界对（族甲 ↔ 族戊，Φ_enc：《场具化构造式》命题五的改名 seed↔pair、flow↔drive、write↔hebb、relax↔decay，α = 1）')
PHI_E2 = {'write': 'hebb', 'relax': 'decay', 'seed': 'pair'}
PHI_I2 = {'cut_flow_bed': 'cut'}
tabJ = {k: dict(v, cut={'cut_flow_bed'} if k == 'write' else set()) for k, v in C.JIA_TABLE.items()}
for name, ok, why in C.mc_check(tabJ, C.WU_TABLE, PHI_E2, C.NODE_MAP_ENC, PHI_I2, F(1, 1000), F(1, 1000), F(1)):
    print(f'  {"成立" if ok else "不成立"}｜{name}｜{why}')
ren = {'seed': 'pair', 'flow': 'drive', 'rest': 'rest'}
n_eq, n_tot, n_pos = 0, 0, 0
for L in range(1, 5):
    for h in itertools.product(('seed', 'flow', 'rest'), repeat=L):
        for t0 in range(L):
            for t1 in range(t0, L):
                for T in ({0}, {1}, {0, 1}):
                    for sw in ({}, {t: {'cut'} for t in range(L)}):
                        a = C.lf_eval(C.JIA, list(h), (t0, t1), T, sw=sw)
                        b = C.lf_eval(C.WU, [ren[x] for x in h], (t0, t1), T, sw=sw)
                        keys = ('Src', 'Ret', 'Part', 'HDyn', 'Q', 'inner')
                        n_tot += 1
                        n_eq += all(a[k] == b[k] for k in keys) and a['traj'] == b['traj']
                        n_pos += a['Q']
print(f'  长度 1—4 的全部史 × 全部窗 × T ∈ {{z1}},{{z2}},{{z1,z2}} × （无干预／全程切断途径）：{n_tot} 组，原子与轨迹逐项相同 {n_eq} 组，其中 Q = 1 者 {n_pos} 组。')
st40, _, ok40 = FJW.wu_run(dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(40), F(40)]), ['pair', 'drive'])
st10, _, ok10 = FJW.wu_run(dict(z=[F(0), F(0)], R=[F(10), F(10)]), ['pair', 'drive'])
e40 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, ['pair', 'drive'], (1, 1), {0})
print(f'  物理层不保存：族戊史 (pair, drive) 受体池初值 40 与 10 编码相同、Q 同为 {e40["Q"]}，物理核验 {ok40} 与 {ok10}（池末态 {C.fx(st10["R"][0], 2)}）——'
      '编码同构不读回物理合法（《场具化构造式》四层分列（丙），本篇保存定理不越过物理层）。')
for m, nm in ((FJW.m_min, 'min'), (FJW.m_mean, 'mean'), (FJW.m_max, 'max')):
    stj = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], L=[F(1, 5), F(0)])
    stw = dict(z=[F(21, 10), F(1, 10)], R=[F(19), F(39)])
    _, c, ext = FJW.Im_jia(stj, m)
    _, c2, feas = FJW.Im_wu(stw, m)
    print(f'  I_{nm}（z = (21/10, 1/10)）：c* = {C.fr(c)}；族甲须与 B_phys 外交换结构物质 {C.fr(ext)}（现申报内不可实现）；族戊 R-池 资源可行 {feas}（设定途径未申报）')
~~~

A.12　run15\_witness.py　入口：纤维见证、GS、超拍编码障碍、率阶梯、MC5-ε、干预交换、实际运行对应不足、单向性、复合与尺度共轭（第十、十一节）

~~~python
"""第十五篇附录程序：失败边界的精确见证与定理的有限核验。
运行：python3 -B run15_witness.py > run15_witness.txt
一 纤维见证（定理 15-8）；二 门控枢轴条件 GS 的充分性（定理 15-8（三））；三 超拍编码障碍（定理 15-10（一），MD-1）；
四 率极限的阶梯性（定理 15-10（二），MD-2）；五 MC5-ε 必要；六 MC4 干预交换；七 实际运行对应不足（MC2-r 必要）与 MC4-𝒟 必要；
八 单向性；九 复合、求逆与 𝒦_g 尺度共轭（定理 15-1—15-3 的正向见证）。
"""
import sys, itertools, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
from deps15 import GC, RW, GK, DC, banner
import core15 as C
banner()
S0 = GC.load_snn_core()[0]
G = GC.snn_gate(S0)
FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * 6
REST = [C.NONE] * 8
T = C.TPOS

def gs(G, o, sig, T, j=0, avail=None):
    """调用 core15.gs（只把可用的 T 位设回基线）。avail 缺省视 T 全部可用（由调用处另核可用性）。"""
    return C.gs(G, o, sig, T, set(T) if avail is None else avail, j)

print('\n一、纤维见证（定理 15-8）：同一冻结转导器 τ 的同一像、T 上轨迹逐拍相同，而 SNN 一侧 HDyn 不同')
WS = {'S_A': [('A', (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE], 'S_AB': [('AB', (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE],
      'S_I': [('A', (F(1, 2), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE]}
res = {}
for nm, wl in WS.items():
    h = FORM + REST + wl
    P, H, rows, I = C.snn_full(nm, G, h, 26)
    ok, vt, vtb = gs(G, I.pre[26], h[26], T, avail={k for k in T if I.is_avail(k, 26)})
    res[nm] = (P, H, I)
    print(f'  {nm}：拍 26 字母 {h[26]}；Part = {P}，HDyn = {H}；GS = {ok}（实际门控实参 {C.fx(vt)}，T 设回基线 {C.fx(vtb)}，θ = 1）')
    print('    作用于 T 的窗内事件：' + '；'.join(f'{a} c={C.fx(c)} a={aa} η={v}({tier})' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows))
same = all(res['S_A'][2].pre[t][0][i][0] == res[n][2].pre[t][0][i][0] and res['S_A'][2].pre[t][2][:2] == res[n][2].pre[t][2][:2]
           and res['S_A'][2].pre[t][3][0] == res[n][2].pre[t][3][0]
           for n in ('S_AB', 'S_I') for t in range(30) for i in (0, 1))
print(f'  三者在 T 位（w00、w10）、已映射入口的资格迹 x0、x1 与输出 0 的后迹 y0 上逐拍相同（拍 0—29）：{same}')

imgs = {nm: tuple(C.tau(FORM + REST + wl)) for nm, wl in WS.items()}
print(f'  三者在冻结转导器 τ 下的像相同：{len(set(imgs.values())) == 1}；像的 pair 拍 {[t for t, x in enumerate(imgs["S_A"]) if x == "pair"]}，末三拍 {imgs["S_A"][-3:]}')
r2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, list(imgs['S_A']), (26, 28), {0})
print(f'  像实例（族戊）：Src = {r2["Src"]}，Ret = {r2["Ret"]}，Part = {r2["Part"]}，HDyn = {r2["HDyn"]}，Q = {r2["Q"]}。'
      '故在这一共同像的 τ-纤维上 HDyn₁ 取 1 与 0：任何以 τ-像为输入的恢复规则在此纤维上必错，任何认证 2→1 保存的条件集须排除此纤维（定理 15-8，只签此纤维）；差别在未映射的门控读取（非 T 位 w20、w30、教学电流 I0）。')
G3 = GC.Gate(G.n_in, G.n_out, G.pat, G.lam, [F(3), F(1)], G.vreset, G.dx, G.dy, G.Ap, G.Am, G.rho, G.b, G.lo, G.hi)
h3 = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
L3 = C.snn_light(G3, h3, 26)
r3 = C.snn_r_M1(G3, h3)
r2b = C.lf_eval(C.WU, C.tau(h3), (26, 43), {0})
print(f'  未映射参数的纤维见证：只把输出 0 的阈改为 θ₀ = 3（τ 像与实例 P 相同）：SNN q = {L3["q"]}，r = {r3["r"]}（作用于 T 的有效写入 {r3["tot"]} 个），'
      f'窗内作用于 T 的有效事件 {len(L3["teff"])} 个 ⇒ HDyn = 0；像实例 Src = {r2b["Src"]}，Ret = {r2b["Ret"]}，HDyn = {r2b["HDyn"]}——q、r、HDyn 在同一像上都不由像决定。')
print('\n二、GS 的充分性（定理 15-8（三）；只设可用位）：GS 成立 ⇒ 同拍把 T 设回基线即关门、PLUS 不发生、读边可达 ⇒ η(PLUS) = 1（有限核验）')
cnt = dict(gs=0, ok=0, nogs=0)
for W0 in (F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)):
    for Ap in (F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)):
        for teach in (F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)):
            for n in (1, 3, 6):
                for X in ('A', 'AB'):
                    S0.W0, S0.A_PLUS = W0, Ap
                    Gx = GC.snn_gate(S0)
                    h = [('A', (teach, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE] * n + [C.NONE] * 8 + [(X, (0, 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE]
                    t = 3 * n + 8
                    L = C.snn_light(Gx, h, t)
                    o = L['pre'][t]
                    ok, vt, vtb = gs(Gx, o, h[t], T, avail={k for k in T if t in L['avail'][k]})
                    if not ok:
                        cnt['nogs'] += 1
                        continue
                    cnt['gs'] += 1
                    av = all(t in L['avail'][k] for k in T)
                    o2 = GK.setw(o, {k: Gx.b[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
                    ev2 = RW.Ev(Gx, o2, h[t], t)
                    plus_gone = ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) in L['evs'][t] and ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) not in ev2
                    reads = ('w', 0, 0) in L['evs'][t][('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]['reads']
                    cnt['ok'] += bool(av and plus_gone and reads)
S0.W0, S0.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
print(f'  参数网格 W0 × A₊ × 教学 × 形成次数 × 窗首字母（A／AB）共 216 例：GS 成立 {cnt["gs"]} 例，其中「T 位可用、设回基线 PLUS 消失、PLUS 读 T 位」{cnt["ok"]} 例；GS 不成立 {cnt["nogs"]} 例（不据此判 η = 0：GS 只是充分条件）。')
print('\n三、超拍编码障碍（定理 15-10（一））：块和 Σ PLUS 作为设定值 σ 的函数分段为常值，块内首个门控切换处的跃变 J 不属 εℤ')
h = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
pre, evs, gates = C.snn_run(G, h)
u = 26

def block(sig_val, L, which='plus'):
    o = GK.setw(pre[u], {('w', 0, 0): sig_val, ('w', 1, 0): sig_val})
    tot = F(0)
    for t in range(u, u + L):
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, h[t], t)
        if which == 'plus':
            tot += e[('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]['c'] if ('PLUS', (0, 0), t) in e else 0
        o, _ = G.step(o, h[t])
    return tot if which == 'plus' else o[0][0][0] - sig_val

N = 280
grid = [G.b[0][0] + (G.hi[0][0] - G.b[0][0]) * F(j, N) for j in range(N + 1)]
for L in (1, 6, 12, 18):
    vals = [block(sv, L) for sv in grid]
    jumps = [j for j in range(N) if vals[j] != vals[j + 1]]
    j0 = jumps[0]
    lo, hi_ = grid[j0], grid[j0 + 1]
    for _ in range(40):
        mid = (lo + hi_) / 2
        if block(mid, L) == vals[j0]:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi_ = mid
    J = block(hi_, L) - block(lo, L)
    Jw = block(hi_, L, 'w') - block(lo, L, 'w')
    print(f'  块长 L = {L:2d}：网格 {N + 1} 点上 Σ PLUS00 取不同值 {len(set(vals))} 个；首个切换点 σ* ∈ ({C.fx(lo, 9)}, {C.fx(hi_, 9)})；'
          f'块和跃变 J = {C.fx(J)}，J/ε = {C.fx(J * 100, 4)}（∉ ℤ：{(J * 100).denominator != 1}）；w00 块增量跃变 {C.fx(Jw)}')
print('\n四、率极限的阶梯性（定理 15-10（二））：常驱动 u、复位到 0 的门控单元，发放周期 n(u) = min{n : u·Σ_{k<n} λ^k ≥ θ}，率 1/n(u)')

def n_of(u_, lam, th=F(1), nmax=400):
    s, p = F(0), F(1)
    for n in range(1, nmax + 1):
        s += p
        p *= lam
        if u_ * s >= th:
            return n
    return None

def sim_rate(u_, lam, L=240, th=F(1)):
    v, cnt_ = F(0), 0
    for _ in range(L):
        vt = lam * v + u_
        if vt >= th:
            cnt_ += 1
            v = F(0)
        else:
            v = vt
    return cnt_

bad = 0
for lam in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)):
    us = [F(j, 200) for j in range(1, 401)]
    rs = []
    for u_ in us:
        n = n_of(u_, lam)
        r = F(0) if n is None else F(1, n)
        c = sim_rate(u_, lam)
        if n is not None and c != 240 // n:
            bad += 1
        rs.append(r)
    distinct = sorted(set(rs))
    jmax = max(rs[i + 1] - rs[i] for i in range(len(rs) - 1))
    mono = all(rs[i] <= rs[i + 1] for i in range(len(rs) - 1))
    print(f'  λ = {C.fr(lam)}：u ∈ (0, 2] 的 400 个网格点上率取 {len(distinct)} 个值，单调不减 {mono}，最大跃变 {C.fr(jmax)}；'
          f'任一仿射律 y = β·u 逼近的一致误差 ≥ 跃变/2 = {C.fr(jmax / 2)}')
print(f'  周期公式与逐拍模拟（240 拍计数 = ⌊240/n(u)⌋）不一致 {bad} 例。')
print('\n五、MC5-ε 必要（定理 15-2）：恒等 Φ（律、状态、事件、途径、干预全同），只改 ε')
hN = [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), C.NONE, C.NONE, C.NONE, ('A', (0, 0))]
for eps in (F(1, 100), F(1, 1000)):
    GK.EPS = eps
    DC.EPS = eps
    P, H, rows, I = C.snn_full('N', G, hN, 1, T=[('w', 0, 0), ('w', 1, 0), ('w', 2, 0), ('w', 3, 0)])
    print(f'  实例 N（第十一篇 7.5（二）），T = T_max 的四位，ε = {C.fr(eps)}：Part = {P}，HDyn = {H}；作用于 T 的事件 '
          + '；'.join(f'{a} c={C.fr(c)} a={aa} η={v}' for a, c, aa, v, tier in rows if a.startswith(('PLUS', 'MINUS'))))
GK.EPS = DC.EPS = F(1, 100)
print('\n六、MC4 干预交换（定理 15-11）')
hP = FORM + REST + GC.WIN0()
img = C.tau(hP)
W = set(range(26, 44))
for lab, sw1, sw2 in (('bap 关（W 内）↦ cut(N→syn)', {t: (True, False) for t in W}, {t: {'cut'} for t in W}),
                      ('bap 关 ↦ cut(N→syn) ∧ 冻结衰减（修正 φ_I）', {t: (True, False) for t in W}, {t: {'cut', 'freeze_rl'} for t in W}),
                      ('plastic 关（W 内）↦ freeze', {t: (False, True) for t in W}, {t: {'freeze'} for t in W})):
    L1 = C.snn_light(G, hP, 26, sw=sw1)
    r2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, img, (26, 43), {0}, sw=sw2)
    hd1 = 0 if not L1['teff'] else '另判'
    print(f'  {lab}：SNN 窗内作用于 T 的有效事件 {len(L1["teff"])} 个 ⇒ HDyn = {hd1}，Part（SYN 承担）= {L1["kappa_syn"]}，q = {L1["q"]}｜'
          f'族戊 HDyn = {r2["HDyn"]}（见证 {r2["witness"][:2]}），Part = {r2["Part"]}，Ret = {r2["Ret"]}')
L1 = C.snn_light(G, hP[:26] + hP[26:], 26)
o26 = L1['pre'][26]
hE = hP
pre1, evs1, _ = C.snn_run(G, hE[:26])
oE = GK.setw(pre1[26], {k: G.b[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
teffE, o = [], oE
for t in range(26, 44):
    e = RW.Ev(G, o, hE[t], t)
    teffE += [x for x in e.values() if x['tgt'] in T and abs(x['c']) > F(1, 100)]
    o, _ = G.step(o, hE[t])
r2E = C.lf_eval(C.WU, img, (26, 43), {0}, erase={26: (0,)})
print(f'  擦除（拍 26 前 w00、w10 ← b ↦ z1 ← 0）：SNN 窗内作用于 T 的有效事件 {len(teffE)} 个（HDyn = 0），窗末 z = {C.fx(o[0][0][0] - G.b[0][0])}｜'
      f'族戊 HDyn = {r2E["HDyn"]}（见证 {r2E["witness"][:3]}），Ret = {r2E["Ret"]}，z1 在拍 26 由 hebb 常数项 b 重新形成：形成记录 {r2E["form"][0][-2:]}')
print('\n七、实际运行对应不足（定理 15-4）')
M0 = dict(C.WU, gam=F(0))
h7 = ['pair', 'rest', 'drive', 'drive']
a = C.lf_eval(M0, h7, (2, 3), {0})
bs = {e['t']: e['c'] for e in a['wev'] if e['kind'] == 'hebb' and e['i'] == 0}
Mc = dict(M0, bsched=bs)
b = C.lf_eval(Mc, h7, (2, 3), {0})
print(f'  （甲）MC2-r：族戊（γ = 0）与把 hebb 改为逐拍常数律（c 取实际值）的变体：z1 轨迹相同 {a["traj"] == b["traj"] or [x[0] for x in a["traj"]] == [x[0] for x in b["traj"]]}，'
      f'MC1、MC3、MC5 成立；HDyn = {a["HDyn"]} 与 {b["HDyn"]}（《场具化数理式》定理一的 HistCond 例在迁移框架中的读法：承继，不计增量）。')
I = res['S_A'][2]
ok_all = True
for u in sorted(set(I.avail[('w', 0, 0)]) & set(range(1, 27))):
    pin = GK.setw(I.pre[u], {k: G.hi[k[1]][k[2]] for k in T})
    _, _, g2 = GK.run_with(G, pin, I.hist[u:29], t_start=u)
    ok_all &= all(g2[t] == I.gates[t] for t in g2)
print(f'  （乙）MC4-𝒟：S_A 的申报干预域由 [b, hi] 改为只上调域 [实际值, hi]（律、运行、读取、途径全同）：高钉位在每一可用拍 u 起重跑至拍 28 的门控与实际逐拍相同 {ok_all}'
      f' ⇒ 由第十一篇引理 C，只上调域内一切设定不改门控，PLUS、MINUS 不变，η = 0；原域 [b, hi] 下 HDyn = {res["S_A"][1]}。')
print('\n八、单向性（P15-2）：φ_E 单射而不满射时只保 HDyn 的正值')
M1 = dict(C.WU, gam=F(0))
x1 = C.lf_eval(M1, ['pair', 'rest', 'rest'], (1, 2), {0})
x2 = C.lf_eval(C.WU, ['pair', 'rest', 'rest'], (1, 2), {0})
~~~
